# SWEEP #10 · TERMINAL 17 — Logins, sign-outs and the gates every request passes
## phases P186001–P187000 (block pre-allocated) · 500 new checks · run 2026-10-08 on port 4417

**Territory:** 32 files — the five sign-in/out routes, `/api/panel-profile`, the three sign-in doors and
their cards, `/r/<slug>/owner`, the blocked screen, `instrumentation*.ts`, `sentry.*.config.ts`, and
`lib/{adminFetch,loginThrottle,managerPin,ownerPin,ownerScope,panelAccess,panelGate,panelScope,panelSettings,passwordVault,publicCap,rateLimit,revealGate,staffAuth,supabaseAdmin,userAuth}.ts`.
One new file was added by fix 1: `lib/sentryPrivacy.ts`.

**Result:** 521 ✅ · 0 ❌ — 500 planned checks, plus P186501–P186521 for the four Part-4 items the owner picked (11–14). P186484 (the note box font) was ❌ and is fixed by item 11.
**Problems found and fixed: 10** (one commit each, every one with a guard that was sabotage-tested) —
see `.claude/sweep/T17-S10-findings.md`.

## How the 500 were aimed — rows per file BY SUBJECT before this run (re-derived, not copied)

0 rows: `instrumentation-client.ts`, `instrumentation.ts`, `lib/revealGate.ts` (new since 2026-09-01) ·
1 row: BlockedView, ownerPin, panel-login, panel-logout, panel-profile, sentry.edge, sentry.server, staff-login page,
staff-login route, staff-logout · 2–3: adminFetch, loginThrottle, staff LoginForm, /login, /r/<slug>/owner,
panelScope, panelSettings, passwordVault, staffAuth · 4–15: managerPin, /r/<slug>/login, panelGate, publicCap,
supabaseAdmin, rateLimit, panelAccess · 33: userAuth · 80: ownerScope. The thin files got the most rows.
**Driven, not read:** of the old rows, the gate every owner route stands on (`lib/ownerScope.ts`) had been
driven by sweep #9; the doors and the profile route had almost no driven rows. Runner B (P186200–P186298) drives
all of them as the five diag people + the admin, on my own port, with ZERO extra sign-ins (cached sessions).

OTHER territories' files (three owner pages show the crash page on a database blip; 27 admin screens do not use adminFetch), each

# ROUND 4 — 2026-10-09, owner: "one latest time … do 600 phases test … every single bit of thing should be covered … within the boundaries"

**Ids: `P161539`–`P162232`** (694 checks, 694 ✅) — the rest of T17's round-3 range and on into the same deliberately free
gap (P160959–P170000; checked empty on main and in every open PR first). The shared *Next free ID* line was not read and not edited.

**How "every bit" was made measurable:** the 23 library and route `.ts` files were run under Node's own code coverage (V8) in a hermetic harness — the real
files against an in-memory database that can fail on purpose, no network (any fetch is refused and recorded). Then every branch the report
said had never run got a check. Final: **line coverage 96.7–100% in each of the 23 (100% in 18), branch coverage 86–100%**; the 4 error-report setup files (`sentry.*.config.ts`, `instrumentation*.ts`) were run by their own suite with a stand-in reporter. The lines
that never run are named, not hidden: the re-throw of an error a programming mistake would cause (panel-profile 51-52, ownerScope 87-90,
userAuth 418-419), `sameSitePath`'s catch (a path starting with "/" cannot make `new URL` throw), and panel-login 146-148 — the
"panel not enabled" refusal, which the 2026-07-31 decision (all panels always on) made unreachable. The five `.tsx` screens cannot run under
Node, so they were driven in a real browser with the server's replies faked per request (56 checks).

**Problems found and fixed: 5** — item 25 (the retired owner switch could still lock an owner out), 26 (a blip while saving your own details
erased the owner's ID record and note), 27 (a blip during a password change said "wrong password"), 28 (a blip crashed the owner cockpit),
29 (admin-door and manager-PIN wrong tries sent at once counted as one; migration 414). One question left to the owner, not changed: a
SUSPENDED restaurant's staff are refused at `/r/<slug>/<panel>` but let in at the plain `/<panel>` (P-row "QUESTION FOR THE OWNER").
